# Vendored ONCE from rofi-emoji 4.1.0-3 (usr/share/rofi-emoji/all_emojis.txt,
# github.com/Mange/rofi-emoji, MIT), whose emoji list is itself copied from
# github.com/Mange/emoji-data. Emoji names, categories and keywords derive
# from Unicode CLDR annotation data, (c) Unicode, Inc. and contributors,
# licensed under CC-BY-4.0 <https://www.unicode.org/copyright.html#Exhibit1>.
# The emoji glyphs themselves are literal UTF-8 Unicode characters, not
# Nerd-Font PUA codepoints -- the shell's PUA policy test does not apply here.
# Format: one entry per line, "<emoji> <searchable text>" -- a single space
# after the emoji, everything after it is free-text (name/category/keywords)
# for the picker's case-insensitive substring filter. Lines starting with
# "#" are comments and are skipped by the parser.
